Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the giants space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 757 and the convergence rate is O(254/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on giants structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 757. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 254. QED.
